app/lib/data/player/providers/miruro/miruro_http2_client.dart:31: /// Minimal HTTP/2 GET transport for the public Miruro backend. /// /// Why this exists: the backend's catalog route is only served over HTTP/2. /// The same host answers `/health` over HTTP/1.1, so this is not a network, /// address, or client block; it is a route rule that the site's own browser /// satisfies by negotiating `h2` through ALPN. Dart's `dart:io` HTTP client is /// HTTP/1.1 only, so the catalog is read through this narrow client instead. /// /// It sends exactly the request the site's own bundle sends: `GET`, no body, no /// cookies, no authorization, and the standard fetch-metadata context. Keep it /// that way: this class is not a general-purpose evasion layer. final class MiruroHttp2Client implements MiruroCatalogTransport

app/lib/data/player/providers/miruro/miruro_media_proxy.dart:9: /// Loopback reverse proxy for Miruro media. /// /// Every resolved stream is published as `http://127.0.0.1:<port>/media/<id>`, /// so the player never holds a backend CDN URL. The proxy is the single place /// that applies the `Referer`/`Origin`/`User-Agent` the backend declares, /// forwards `Range`, and preserves `206` with `Content-Range`. That keeps seek /// correct, hides mirror and token rotation from the engine, and lets the source /// appear as an internal, labelled stream. /// /// Scope: public `GET` only, loopback only, no request body, no cookies, no /// authorization. Sub-resources are addressed by an opaque id plus the base64url /// of their absolute upstream URL, which is validated against the registered /// upstream host family before any request leaves the process. final class MiruroMediaProxy
